Outputs JSON + Markdown.\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport re\\\\nimport sys\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom scipy import stats\\\\n\\\\n# ── Configuration \\\\\\\"embedding_distance\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"dataflow_aware\\\\\\\"]\\\\nSEEDS = [42, 43, 44]\\\\n\\\\nCHECKPOINT_BASE = \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/checkpoints\\\\\\\"\\\\nOUTPUT_BASE = \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_outputs\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n# StarCoder s42 used 3 epochs (others used 2)\\\\nHETEROGENEITY_NOTES = {\\\\n    (\\\\\\\"starcoder\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"random\\\\\\\", 42): \\\\\\\"3 epochs (others: 2)\\\\\\\",\\\\n    (\\\\\\\"starcoder\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"embedding_distance\\\\\\\", 42): \\\\\\\"3 epochs (others: 2)\\\\\\\",\\\\n    (\\\\\\\"starcoder\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"dataflow_aware\\\\\\\", 42): \\\\\\\"3 epochs (others: 2)\\\\\\\",\\\\n    (\\\\\\\"qwen\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"random\\\\\\\", 42): \\\\\\\"3 epochs (others: 2)\\\\\\\",\\\\n    (\\\\\\\"qwen\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"embedding_distance\\\\\\\", 42): \\\\\\\"3 epochs (others: 2)\\\\\\\",\\\\n    (\\\\\\\"qwen\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"dataflow_aware\\\\\\\", 42): \\\\\\\"3 epochs (others: 2)\\\\\\\",\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _candidate_dirs(encoder: str, strategy: str, seed: int) -> List[str]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return candidate directories to search for a run's output.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    enc_short = {\\\\\\\"qwen\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"qwen\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"starcoder\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"starcoder\\\\\\\"}[encoder]\\\\n    enc_full = {\\\\\\\"qwen\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"m2_full_qwen\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"starcoder\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"m2_full_starcoder\\\\\\\"}[encoder]\\\\n    tag = f\\\\\\\"{strategy}_s{seed}\\\\\\\"\\\\n    tag_enc = f\\\\\\\"{strategy}_{enc_short}_s{seed}\\\\\\\"\\\\n    return [\\\\n        f\\\\\\\"{OUTPUT_BASE}/{tag}\\\\\\\",\\\\n        f\\\\\\\"{OUTPUT_BASE}/{tag_enc}\\\\\\\",\\\\n        f\\\\\\\"{CHECKPOINT_BASE}/{enc_full}/{tag}\\\\\\\",\\\\n    ]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _parse_best_r1_from_log(log_path: str) -> Optional[float]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extract the last 'New best R@1=X.XXXX' value from a train.log.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    best = None\\\\n    pattern = re.compile(r\\\\\\\"New best R@1=([\\\\\\\\d.]+)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    with open(log_path) as f:\\\\n        for line in f:\\\\n            m = pattern.search(line)\\\\n            if m:\\\\n                best = float(m.group(1))\\\\n    return best\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _parse_eval_metrics_from_log(log_path: str) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Parse all [eval@step] lines, return list of metric dicts.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    results = []\\\\n    pattern = re.compile(r\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\[eval@(\\\\\\\\d+)\\\\\\\\]\\\\\\\\s*(\\\\\\\\{.*\\\\\\\\})\\\\\\\")\\\\n    with open(log_path) as f:\\\\n        for line in f:\\\\n            m = pattern.search(line)\\\\n            if m:\\\\n                step = int(m.group(1))\\\\n                metrics_str = m.group(2).replace(\\\\\\\"'\\\\\\\", '\\\\\\\"')\\\\n                try:\\\\n                    metrics = json.loads(metrics_str)\\\\n                    metrics[\\\\\\\"step\\\\\\\"] = step\\\\n                    results.append(metrics)\\\\n                except json.JSONDecodeError:\\\\n                    pass\\\\n    return results\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _parse_final_eval(log_path: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, float]]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Parse [Final eval] line.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    pattern = re.compile(r\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\[Final eval\\\\\\\\]\\\\\\\\s*(\\\\\\\\{.*\\\\\\\\})\\\\\\\")\\\\n    with open(log_path) as f:\\\\n        for line in f:\\\\n            m = pattern.search(line)\\\\n            if m:\\\\n                metrics_str = m.group(1).replace(\\\\\\\"'\\\\\\\", '\\\\\\\"')\\\\n                try:\\\\n                    return json.loads(metrics_str)\\\\n                except json.JSONDecodeError:\\\\n                    pass\\\\n    return None\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef collect_metrics(dry_run: bool = False) -> Dict[Tuple[str, str, int], Dict]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Collect best R@1 for all 18 runs. Returns {(encoder, strategy, seed): info}.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    results = {}\\\\n\\\\n    if dry_run:\\\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(2026)\\\\n        base_values = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"random\\\\\\\": 0.42,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"embedding_distance\\\\\\\": 0.48,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"dataflow_aware\\\\\\\": 0.51,\\\\n        }\\\\n        encoder_offsets = {\\\\\\\"qwen\\\\\\\": 0.03, \\\\\\\"starcoder\\\\\\\": 0.0}\\\\n        for encoder in ENCODERS:\\\\n            for strategy in STRATEGIES:\\\\n                for seed in SEEDS:\\\\n                    base = base_values[strategy] + encoder_offsets[encoder]\\\\n                    noise = rng.normal(0, 0.015)\\\\n                    r1 = round(float(np.clip(base + noise, 0, 1)), 4)\\\\n                   \\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 10596 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\narisons,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"raw_runs\\\\\\\": raw_serializable,\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ─�� Main ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\\\n\\\\ndef main():\\\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\\\\\"M2 statistical analysis\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\n        \\\\\\\"--dry-run\\\\\\\", action=\\\\\\\"store_true\\\\\\\",\\\\n        help=\\\\\\\"Use mock data to verify script logic\\\\\\\",\\\\n    )\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\n        \\\\\\\"--output-dir\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\"/root/structfuse/results\\\\\\\",\\\\n        help=\\\\\\\"Directory to write result files\\\\\\\",\\\\n    )\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\n        \\\\\\\"--bootstrap-iter\\\\\\\", type=int, default=1000,\\\\n        help=\\\\\\\"Number of bootstrap iterations\\\\\\\",\\\\n    )\\\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"M2 Statistical Analysis {'[DRY-RUN]' if args.dry_run else ''}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"Design: {len(ENCODERS)} encoders x {len(STRATEGIES)} strategies x {len(SEEDS)} seeds = {len(ENCODERS)*len(STRATEGIES)*len(SEEDS)} runs\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # 1. Collect metrics\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(\\\"Phase 1: Collecting metrics\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    metrics = collect_metrics(dry_run=args.dry_run)\\n\\n    found = sum(1 for v in metrics.values() if v[\\\"best_r1\\\"] is not None)\\n    missing = sum(1 for v in metrics.values() if v[\\\"best_r1\\\"] is None)\\n    print(f\\\"  Found: {found}/{len(metrics)} runs with R@1 values\\\")\\n    if missing > 0:\\n        print(f\\\"  Missing: {missing} runs\\\")\\n        for (enc, strat, seed), info in sorted(metrics.items()):\\n            if info[\\\"best_r1\\\"] is None:\\n                print(f\\\"    - {enc}/{strat}/s{seed}: {info['note']}\\\")\\n\\n    print()\\n    for (enc, strat, seed), info in sorted(metrics.items()):\\n        r1_str = f\\\"{info['best_r1']:.4f}\\\" if info['best_r1'] is not None else \\\"MISSING\\\"\\n        flag = \\\" *\\\" if (enc, strat, seed) in HETEROGENEITY_NOTES else \\\"\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"  {enc:12s} {strat:22s} s{seed}: R@1={r1_str}{flag}\\\")\\n\\n    # 2. Per-condition summary\\n    print()\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(\\\"Phase 2: Per-condition summary\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    summary = per_condition_summary(metrics)\\n    for s in summary:\\n        if s[\\\"mean\\\"] is not None:\\n            print(f\\\"  {s['encoder']:12s} {s['strategy']:22s}: {s['mean']:.4f} +/- {s['std']:.4f} (n={s['n']})\\\")\\n        else:\\n            print(f\\\"  {s['encoder']:12s} {s['strategy']:22s}: NO DATA\\\")\\n\\n    # 3. Statistical comparisons\\n    print()\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(\\\"Phase 3: Statistical comparisons\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    comparisons = run_comparisons(metrics)\\n\\n    for label, c in comparisons.items():\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n  [{label}]\\\")\\n        if c[\\\"status\\\"] != \\\"OK\\\":\\n            print(f\\\"    WARN: {c['status']}: only {c['n_pairs']} valid pairs\\\")\\n            continue\\n        print(f\\\"    Treatment: {_fmt(c['treatment_mean'])} +/- {_fmt(c['treatment_std'])}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"    Control:   {_fmt(c['control_mean'])} +/- {_fmt(c['control_std'])}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"    D Mean:    {_fmt(c['mean_diff'])}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"    Paired t:  t={_fmt(c['paired_ttest']['t_statistic'])}, p={_fmt(c['paired_ttest']['p_value'])}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"    Wilcoxon:  W={_fmt(c['wilcoxon']['w_statistic'])}, p={_fmt(c['wilcoxon']['p_value'])}\\\")\\n        ci = c[\\\"bootstrap_95ci\\\"]\\n        print(f\\\"    Boot 95CI: [{_fmt(ci['lower'])}, {_fmt(ci['upper'])}]\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"    Cohen's d: {_fmt(c['cohens_d'])}\\\")\\n        if c.get(\\\"heterogeneity_notes\\\"):\\n            for n in c[\\\"heterogeneity_notes\\\"]:\\n                print(f\\\"    Note: {n}\\\")\\n\\n    # 4. Save outputs\\n    out_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    suffix = \\\"_dryrun\\\" if args.dry_run else \\\"\\\"\\n\\n    json_path = out_dir / f\\\"m2_stats{suffix}.json\\\"\\n    json_data = format_json_results(summary, comparisons, metrics)\\n    with open(json_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(json_data, f, indent=2)\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nJSON saved: {json_path}\\\")\\n\\n    md_path = out_dir / f\\\"m2_stats{suffix}.md\\\"\\n    md_text = format_markdown(summary, comparisons)\\n    with open(md_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        f.write(md_text)\\n    print(f\\\"Markdown saved: {md_path}\\\")\\n\\n    print(\\\"\\\\nDone.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}}}

\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"StructFuse contrastive bi-encoder with fusion head.\\\\n\\\\nBi-encoder over Qwen2.5-Coder-3B with InfoNCE loss, weighted positives\\\\n(data-flow path distance), and an optional lightweight fusion head that\\\\ncombines embedding similarity with explicit structural features.\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoModel, AutoConfig\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass FusionHead(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"MLP that fuses embedding similarity with explicit features into a relevance score.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    FEATURE_NAMES = [\\\\n        \\\\\\\"dataflow_distance\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"bm25_score\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"co_change_freq\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"inter_procedural_flag\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"path_count\\\\\\\",\\\\n    ]\\\\n    NUM_EXPLICIT = len(FEATURE_NAMES)\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int = 128):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        input_dim = 1 + self.NUM_EXPLICIT  # embed_similarity + explicit features\\\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\\\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\\\\n            nn.GELU(),\\\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim),\\\\n            nn.GELU(),\\\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1),\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, embed_similarity: torch.Tensor, features: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            embed_similarity: (B,) or (B, K) cosine similarities.\\\\n            features: same leading dims + NUM_EXPLICIT trailing dim.\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            scores with same shape as embed_similarity.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        orig_shape = embed_similarity.shape\\\\n        sim = embed_similarity.unsqueeze(-1)  # (..., 1)\\\\n        x = torch.cat([sim, features], dim=-1)  # (..., 1+NUM_EXPLICIT)\\\\n        x = x.view(-1, x.size(-1))\\\\n        out = self.net(x).squeeze(-1)\\\\n        return out.view(orig_shape)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass StructFuseEncoder(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Bi-encoder with shared Qwen backbone, optional projection & LoRA.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        model_name: str = \\\\\\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-3B\\\\\\\",\\\\n        proj_dim: int = 256,\\\\n        use_projection: bool = True,\\\\n        use_lora: bool = False,\\\\n        lora_r: int = 16,\\\\n        lora_alpha: int = 32,\\\\n        use_fusion_head: bool = False,\\\\n        fusion_hidden: int = 128,\\\\n        init_temperature: float = 0.07,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.backbone = AutoModel.from_pretrained(model_name, trust_remote_code=True, dtype=torch.float16)\\\\n        hidden_size = self.backbone.config.hidden_size\\\\n\\\\n        self.use_projection = use_projection\\\\n        if use_projection:\\\\n            self.projection = nn.Sequential(\\\\n                nn.Linear(hidden_size, hidden_size),\\\\n                nn.GELU(),\\\\n                nn.Linear(hidden_size, proj_dim),\\\\n            )\\\\n            self.embed_dim = proj_dim\\\\n        else:\\\\n            self.projection = None\\\\n            self.embed_dim = hidden_size\\\\n\\\\n        self.use_fusion_head = use_fusion_head\\\\n        if use_fusion_head:\\\\n            self.fusion_head = FusionHead(hidden_dim=fusion_hidden)\\\\n\\\\n        # learnable temperature for InfoNCE\\\\n        self.log_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(math.log(init_temperature)))\\\\n\\\\n        if use_lora:\\\\n            self._apply_lora(lora_r, lora_alpha)\\\\n\\\\n    def _apply_lora(self, r: int, alpha: int):\\\\n        from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model\\\\n\\\\n        lora_cfg = LoraConfig(\\\\n            r=r,\\\\n            lora_alpha=alpha,\\\\n            target_modules=[\\\\\\\"q_proj\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"v_proj\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            lora_dropout=0.05,\\\\n            bias=\\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\",\\\\n            task_type=\\\\\\\"FEATURE_EXTRACTION\\\\\\\",\\\\n        )\\\\n        self.backbone = get_peft_model(self.backbone, lora_cfg)\\\\n\\\\n    def enable_gradient_checkpointing(self):\\\\n        if hasattr(self.backbone, 'enable_input_require_grads'):\\\\n            self.backbone.enable_input_require_grads()\\\\n        self.backbone.gradient_checkpointing_enable()\\\\n\\\\n    @property\\\\n    def temperature(self) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        return self.log_temperature.exp()\\\\n\\\\n    def encode(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor, attention_mask: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Mean-pool last hidden states (excluding padding) and optionally project.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        outputs = self.backbone(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\\\n        hidden = outputs.last_hidden_state  # (B, L, H)\\\\n\\\\n        mask_expanded = attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()  # (B, L, 1)\\\\n        summed = (hidden * mask_expanded).sum(dim=1)  # (B, H)\\\\n        lengths = mask_expanded.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1e-9)  # (B, 1)\\\\n        pooled = summed / lengths  # (B, H)\\\\n\\\\n        if self.projection is not None:\\\\n            pooled = self.projection(pooled)\\\\n\\\\n        return F.normalize(pooled, p=2, dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        query_ids: torch.Tensor,\\\\n        query_mask: torch.Tensor,\\\\n        pos_ids: torch.Tensor,\\\\n        pos_mask: torch.Tensor,\\\\n        neg_ids: torch.Tensor,\\\\n        neg_mask: torch.Tensor,\\\\n        pos_weights: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\\\n        features: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\\\n        neg_valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\\\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Compute InfoNCE loss with weighted positives and optional fusion head.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            query_ids/mask: (B, Lq)\\\\n            pos_ids/mask:   (B, Lp)       — one positive per query\\\\n            neg_ids/mask:   (B, K, Ln)    — K explicit negatives per query\\\\n            pos_weights:    (B,)          — exp(-alpha * path_distance) weights\\\\n            features:       (B, 1+K, NUM_EXPLICIT) — features for pos + negs if fusion head is used\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            loss: scalar\\\\n            logits: (B, 1+K) similarity scores (for monitoring)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        B = query_ids.size(0)\\\\n        K = neg_ids.size(1)\\\\n\\\\n        q_emb = self.encode(query_ids, query_mask)  # (B, D)\\\\n        p_emb = self.encode(pos_ids, pos_mask)  # (B, D)\\\\n\\\\n        # flatten negatives for encoding, then reshape back\\\\n        neg_ids_flat = neg_ids.view(B * K, -1)\\\\n        neg_mask_flat = neg_mask.view(B * K, -1)\\\\n        n_emb = self.encode(neg_ids_flat, neg_mask_flat).view(B, K, -1)  # (B, K, D)\\\\n\\\\n        # cosine similarities\\\\n        pos_sim = (q_emb * p_emb).sum(dim=-1)  # (B,)\\\\n        neg_sim = torch.bmm(n_emb, q_emb.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)  # (B, K)\\\\n\\\\n        if self.use_fusion_head and features is not None:\\\\n            all_sim = torch.cat([pos_sim.unsqueeze(1), neg_sim], dim=1)  # (B, 1+K)\\\\n            logits = self.fusion_head(all_sim, features) / self.temperature\\\\n        else:\\\\n            logits = torch.cat([pos_sim.unsqueeze(1), neg_sim], dim=1) / self.temperature  # (B, 1+K)\\\\n\\\\n        # Mask invalid negatives (padding slots from empty candidates)\\\\n        if neg_valid_mask is not None:\\\\n            invalid = ~neg_valid_mask\\\\n            logits[:, 1:] = logits[:, 1:].masked_fill(invalid, float('-inf'))\\\\n\\\\n        # InfoNCE: labels are always index 0 (the positive)\\\\n        labels = torch.zeros(B, dtype=torch.long, device=logits.device)\\\\n\\\\n        if pos_weights is not None:\\\\n            # Weight per-sample loss, not logits — scaling logits inverts the intended effect\\\\n            per_sample_loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels, reduction='none')\\\\n            loss = (per_sample_loss * pos_weights).mean()\\\\n        else:\\\\n            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels)\\\\n\\\\n        return loss, logits\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", self.fusion_head = FusionHead(hidden_dim=fusion_hidden)\\n\\n        # learnable temperature for InfoNCE\\n        self.log_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(math.log(init_temperature)))\\n\\n        if use_lora:\\n            self._apply_lora(lora_r, lora_alpha)\\n\\n    def _apply_lora(self, r: int, alpha: int):\\n        from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model\\n\\n        lora_cfg = LoraConfig(\\n            r=r,\\n            lora_alpha=alpha,\\n            target_modules=[\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\"],\\n            lora_dropout=0.05,\\n            bias=\\\"none\\\",\\n            task_type=\\\"FEATURE_EXTRACTION\\\",\\n        )\\n        self.backbone = get_peft_model(self.backbone, lora_cfg)\\n\\n    def enable_gradient_checkpointing(self):\\n        if hasattr(self.backbone, 'enable_input_require_grads'):\\n            self.backbone.enable_input_require_grads()\\n        self.backbone.gradient_checkpointing_enable()\\n\\n    @property\\n    def temperature(self) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.log_temperature.exp()\\n\\n    def encode(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor, attention_mask: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Mean-pool last hidden states (excluding padding) and optionally project.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        outputs = self.backbone(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\n        hidden = outputs.last_hidden_state  # (B, L, H)\\n\\n        mask_expanded = attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()  # (B, L, 1)\\n        summed = (hidden * mask_expanded).sum(dim=1)  # (B, H)\\n        lengths = mask_expanded.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1e-9)  # (B, 1)\\n        pooled = summed / lengths  # (B, H)\\n\\n        if self.projection is not None:\\n            pooled = self.projection(pooled)\\n\\n        return F.normalize(pooled, p=2, dim=-1)\\n\\n    def forward(\\n        self,\\n        query_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n        query_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n        pos_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n        pos_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n        neg_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n        neg_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n        pos_weights: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\n        features: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\n        neg_valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Compute InfoNCE loss with weighted positives and optional fusion head.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            query_ids/mask: (B, Lq)\\n            pos_ids/mask:   (B, Lp)       — one positive per query\\n            neg_ids/mask:   (B, K, Ln)    — K explicit negatives per query\\n            pos_weights:    (B,)          — exp(-alpha * path_distance) weights\\n            features:       (B, 1+K, NUM_EXPLICIT) — features for pos + negs if fusion head is used\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            loss: scalar\\n            logits: (B, 1+K) similarity scores (for monitoring)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        B = query_ids.size(0)\\n        K = neg_ids.size(1)\\n\\n        q_emb = self.encode(query_ids, query_mask)  # (B, D)\\n        p_emb = self.encode(pos_ids, pos_mask)  # (B, D)\\n\\n        # flatten negatives for encoding, then reshape back\\n        neg_ids_flat = neg_ids.view(B * K, -1)\\n        neg_mask_flat = neg_mask.view(B * K, -1)\\n        n_emb = self.encode(neg_ids_flat, neg_mask_flat).view(B, K, -1)  # (B, K, D)\\n\\n        # cosine similarities\\n        pos_sim = (q_emb * p_emb).sum(dim=-1)  # (B,)\\n        neg_sim = torch.bmm(n_emb, q_emb.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)  # (B, K)\\n\\n        if self.use_fusion_head and features is not None:\\n            all_sim = torch.cat([pos_sim.unsqueeze(1), neg_sim], dim=1)  # (B, 1+K)\\n            logits = self.fusion_head(all_sim, features) / self.temperature\\n        else:\\n            logits = torch.cat([pos_sim.unsqueeze(1), neg_sim], dim=1) / self.temperature  # (B, 1+K)\\n\\n        # Mask invalid negatives (padding slots from empty candidates)\\n        if neg_valid_mask is not None:\\n            invalid = ~neg_valid_mask\\n            logits[:, 1:] = logits[:, 1:].masked_fill(invalid, float('-inf'))\\n\\n        # InfoNCE: labels are always index 0 (the positive)\\n        labels = torch.zeros(B, dtype=torch.long, device=logits.device)\\n\\n        if pos_weights is not None:\\n            # Weight per-sample loss, not logits — scaling logits inverts the intended effect\\n            per_sample_loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels, reduction='none')\\n            loss = (per_sample_loss * pos_weights).mean()\\n        else:\\n            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels)\\n\\n        return loss, logits\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01MGvvAozXYYo4etrBE5Qss8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RqUrzhFhKCTEvvizdckAik","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"bjb1-42106","command":"cat > /root/structfuse/scripts/bootstrap_paired_test.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n\"\"\"\nInstance-level cluster bootstrap: embedding_distance vs random mining (s43).\nEvaluates both checkpoints on val_random_s43.jsonl (common eval set),\ncomputes per-instance R@1, and runs cluster bootstrap clustered by repo.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/structfuse/training\")\nfrom contrastive_model import StructFuseEncoder\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\nRANDOM_CKPT = \"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/checkpoints/m2_full_qwen/random_s43/best_by_recall_at_1\"\nEMBED_CKPT = \"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/checkpoints/m2_full_qwen/embedding_distance_s43/best_by_recall_at_1\"\nBASE_MODEL = \"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/models/Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-3B\"\nVAL_FILE = \"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_training_data/val_random_s43.jsonl\"\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nMAX_SEQ_LEN = 2048\nNUM_NEGATIVES = 7\nENCODE_BATCH = 8\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\nOUTPUT_PATH = \"/root/structfuse/results/bootstrap_s43_paired.json\"\n\n\ndef load_model(ckpt_dir, base_model, device):\n    config_path = Path(ckpt_dir) / \"training_config.json\"\n    with open(config_path) as f:\n        cfg = json.load(f)\n    model = StructFuseEncoder(\n        model_name=base_model,\n        proj_dim=cfg.get(\"proj_dim\", 256),\n        use_lora=cfg.get(\"use_lora\", True),\n        lora_r=cfg.get(\"lora_r\", 16),\n        use_fusion_head=cfg.get(\"use_fusion_head\", False),\n    )\n    state_dict = torch.load(Path(ckpt_dir) / \"model.pt\", map_location=\"cpu\")\n    result = model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)\n    assert not result.missing_keys, f\"Missing keys: {result.missing_keys}\"\n    model = model.to(device).eval()\n    return model\n\n\ndef load_val_data(val_file, num_negatives):\n    samples = []\n    with open(val_file) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            if not line.strip():\n                continue\n            d = json.loads(line)\n            neg_candidates = d.get(\"neg_candidates\", [])\n            candidate_tokens = d.get(\"candidate_tokens\", {})\n            neg_texts = []\n            for cid in neg_candidates:\n                t = candidate_tokens.get(cid, \"\")\n                if t:\n                    neg_texts.append(t)\n                if len(neg_texts) >= num_negatives:\n                    break\n            pool_texts = [d[\"pos_tokens\"]] + neg_texts\n            samples.append({\n                \"query_id\": d[\"query_id\"],\n                \"query_text\": d[\"query_tokens\"],\n                \"pool_texts\": pool_texts,\n            })\n    return samples\n\n\ndef batch_encode(model, tokenizer, texts, device, max_len, batch_size):\n    all_embs = []\n    for i in range(0, len(texts), batch_size):\n        batch = texts[i:i+batch_size]\n        enc = tokenizer(batch, truncation=True, max_length=max_len,\n                       padding=True, return_tensors=\"pt\")\n        enc = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in enc.items()}\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            emb = model.encode(enc[\"input_ids\"], enc[\"attention_mask\"])\n        all_embs.append(emb.cpu())\n    return torch.cat(all_embs, dim=0)\n\n\ndef compute_per_instance_r1(model, tokenizer, samples, device, max_len, batch_size):\n    all_query_texts = [s[\"query_text\"] for s in samples]\n    all_pool_texts = []\n    pool_offsets = []\n    offset = 0\n    for s in samples:\n        pool = s[\"pool_texts\"]\n        all_pool_texts.extend(pool)\n        pool_offsets.append((offset, offset + len(pool)))\n        offset += len(pool)\n\n    print(f\"  Encoding {len(all_query_texts)} queries...\", flush=True)\n    t0 = time.time()\n    query_embs = batch_encode(model, tokenizer, all_query_texts, device, max_len, batch_size)\n    print(f\"  Queries done in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    print(f\"  Encoding {len(all_pool_texts)} pool texts...\", flush=True)\n    t0 = time.time()\n    pool_embs = batch_encode(model, tokenizer, all_pool_texts, device, max_len, batch_size)\n    print(f\"  Pool done in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    r1_list = []\n    for i, (start, end) in enumerate(pool_offsets):\n        q_emb = query_embs[i:i+1]\n        p_embs = pool_embs[start:end]\n        sims = F.cosine_similarity(q_emb, p_embs, dim=1)\n        top_idx = sims.argmax().item()\n        r1_list.append(1 if top_idx == 0 else 0)\n\n    return r1_list\n\n\ndef extract_repo(query_id):\n    return query_id.split(\".\")[0]\n\n\ndef cluster_bootstrap(deltas, repo_ids, n_iterations=10000, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    repo_to_indices = defaultdict(list)\n    for i, repo in enumerate(repo_ids):\n        repo_to_indices[repo].append(i)\n\n    repos = list(repo_to_indices.keys())\n    n_repos = len(repos)\n    deltas = np.array(deltas, dtype=np.float64)\n\n    repo_means = {}\n    repo_sizes = {}\n    for repo in repos:\n        idx = repo_to_indices[repo]\n        repo_means[repo] = deltas[idx].mean()\n        repo_sizes[repo] = len(idx)\n\n    bootstrap_means = np.empty(n_iterations)\n    for b in range(n_iterations):\n        sampled = rng.choice(repos, size=n_repos, replace=True)\n        total_sum = 0.0\n        total_n = 0\n        for repo in sampled:\n            total_sum += repo_means[repo] * repo_sizes[repo]\n            total_n += repo_sizes[repo]\n        bootstrap_means[b] = total_sum / total_n\n\n    mean_delta = float(deltas.mean())\n    ci_lower = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_means, 2.5))\n    ci_upper = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_means, 97.5))\n    p_one_sided = float(np.mean(bootstrap_means <= 0))\n    p_two_sided = float(2 * min(np.mean(bootstrap_means <= 0), np.mean(bootstrap_means >= 0)))\n\n    return {\n        \"mean_delta\": mean_delta,\n        \"ci_95\": [ci_lower, ci_upper],\n        \"p_value_one_sided\": p_one_sided,\n        \"p_value_two_sided\": p_two_sided,\n        \"bootstrap_std\": float(bootstrap_means.std()),\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"Loading val data...\", flush=True)\n    samples = load_val_data(VAL_FILE, NUM_NEGATIVES)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(samples)} instances\", flush=True)\n\n    pool_sizes = [len(s[\"pool_texts\"]) for s in samples]\n    print(f\"Pool sizes: min={min(pool_sizes)}, max={max(pool_sizes)}, mean={np.mean(pool_sizes):.1f}\", flush=True)\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(BASE_MODEL, trust_remote_code=True)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n\n    # Model 1: random_s43\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n    print(\"Model 1: random_s43\", flush=True)\n    model = load_model(RANDOM_CKPT, BASE_MODEL, DEVICE)\n    r1_random = compute_per_instance_r1(model, tokenizer, samples, DEVICE, MAX_SEQ_LEN, ENCODE_BATCH)\n    print(f\"R@1 = {np.mean(r1_random):.4f}\", flush=True)\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    # Model 2: embedding_distance_s43\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n    print(\"Model 2: embedding_distance_s43\", flush=True)\n    model = load_model(EMBED_CKPT, BASE_MODEL, DEVICE)\n    r1_embed = compute_per_instance_r1(model, tokenizer, samples, DEVICE, MAX_SEQ_LEN, ENCODE_BATCH)\n    print(f\"R@1 = {np.mean(r1_embed):.4f}\", flush=True)\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    # Paired analysis\n    query_ids = [s[\"query_id\"] for s in samples]\n    repo_ids = [extract_repo(qid) for qid in query_ids]\n    deltas = [e - r for e, r in zip(r1_embed, r1_random)]\n    unique_repos = sorted(set(repo_ids))\n\n    n_better = sum(1 for d in deltas if d > 0)\n    n_worse = sum(1 for d in deltas if d < 0)\n    n_tied = sum(1 for d in deltas if d == 0)\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n    print(\"Paired Analysis\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Instances: {len(deltas)}, Repos: {len(unique_repos)}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Embedding better: {n_better}, worse: {n_worse}, tied: {n_tied}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"R@1 random:    {np.mean(r1_random):.4f}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"R@1 embedding: {np.mean(r1_embed):.4f}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Mean delta:    {np.mean(deltas):.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n    print(f\"\\nCluster bootstrap ({N_BOOTSTRAP} iterations)...\", flush=True)\n    boot = cluster_bootstrap(deltas, repo_ids, N_BOOTSTRAP)\n\n    crosses_zero = boot[\"ci_95\"][0] <= 0 <= boot[\"ci_95\"][1]\n\n    output = {\n        \"method\": f\"cluster bootstrap (repo-level), {N_BOOTSTRAP} iterations\",\n        \"eval_set\": VAL_FILE,\n        \"n_instances\": len(deltas),\n        \"n_repos\": len(unique_repos),\n        \"R@1_random\": float(np.mean(r1_random)),\n        \"R@1_embedding\": float(np.mean(r1_embed)),\n        \"instance_counts\": {\"embedding_better\": n_better, \"embedding_worse\": n_worse, \"tied\": n_tied},\n        \"mean_delta_R@1\": boot[\"mean_delta\"],\n        \"ci_95\": boot[\"ci_95\"],\n        \"p_value\": boot[\"p_value_one_sided\"],\n        \"p_value_two_sided\": boot[\"p_value_two_sided\"],\n        \"bootstrap_std\": boot[\"bootstrap_std\"],\n        \"crosses_zero\": crosses_zero,\n        \"conclusion\": \"NOT statistically significant (CI crosses zero)\" if crosses_zero else \"Statistically significant (CI does not cross zero)\",\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(time.time() - t_start, 1),\n    }\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n    print(\"RESULT\", flush=True)\n    print(json.dumps(output, indent=2), flush=True)\n\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUTPUT_PATH), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(OUTPUT_PATH, \"w\") as f:\n {\"query_id\": query_ids[i], \"repo\": repo_ids[i],\n         \"r1_random\": r1_random[i], \"r1_embedding\": r1_embed[i], \"delta\": deltas[i]}\n        for i in range(len(samples))\n    ]\n    with main()\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"Script written
